### Part c30.1 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Identify RuPay's origin, domestic applications and overseas milestones. · how: Separate brand construction, card provisions, credit linkage and country-specific activities. · when: Launch in 2012; overseas issuance milestones in 2020 and 2024. · conditions: Acceptance does not imply issuance or universal availability. · atmosphere: A cardholder using an Indian payment network at home and abroad._
_skipped steps: 5: There is no formula; the brand-name expression joins words rather than quantities._

**b2 · explain · step 1: the fact in one line** — Card पर RuPay देखा है? Rupee और Payment के शुरुआती टुकड़े जोड़िए, जैसे नाम की puzzle: RuPay। यह पैसों की equation नहीं; NPCI ने इसे 2012 में launch किया।
  _screen: Join word fragments: Rupee + Payment → RuPay; NPCI, 2012._

**b5 · explain · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — विदेश में card मिलना और चलना समान हैं? Bhutan: पहला overseas issuance milestone 2020, साथ में ATM और merchant acceptance। Mauritius: issuance 2024 में शुरू, यानी नया card देना।
  _screen: Bhutan: 2020 issuance + acceptance. Mauritius: 2024 issuance._

**b6 · explain · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — Nepal और Maldives में कहाँ चलेगा? दोनों में ATM और PoS acceptance है: cash निकालने की machine और दुकान का payment counter। इन entries में issuance year नहीं है।
  _screen: Nepal and Maldives: ATM + point-of-sale acceptance; no issuance year._

**b7 · explain · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — United Arab Emirates और Singapore में क्या समान है? ATM और merchant-terminal acceptance, यानी cash machine और दुकान के card reader पर इस्तेमाल; issuance date नहीं दी गई।
  _screen: UAE and Singapore: ATM + merchant-terminal acceptance._

**b7a · explain · step 3: where it comes from (book or outside, dated)** — ये dates issuance की हैं, total usage की नहीं।
  _screen: NON-SPOKEN PROVENANCE: supplied BIE source, p015-i14: Bhutan overseas issuance milestone, 2020; p015-i16: Mauritius issuance began, 2024. Supplied source only; no independent statistical series provided._

**b8 · recap · step 4: what it changes or proves** — Card type, country की activity और year अलग पहचानिए।
  _screen: Three separate recall fields: card type | activity | date. Non-spoken provenance: supplied BIE source, p015-i7, i11–i19; dates are supplied milestones, not independently updated statistics._

### Part c30.2 · comparison
_frame: process: Compare RuPay with familiar international payment brands. · how: Use function, domestic identity and qualitative fees as separate criteria. · when: When interpreting brand comparisons and fee claims. · conditions: No operational comparison, fee schedule or ranking metric is supplied. · atmosphere: Payment-brand names displayed on cards and checkout counters._

**b9 · compare · step 1: what is common** — Card पर अलग brands क्यों दिखते हैं? RuPay, Mastercard, Visa और China का Union Pay समान card-payment role वाली व्यवस्थाएँ हैं।
  _screen: RuPay | Mastercard | Visa | Union Pay._

**b10 · compare · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — RuPay Indian domestic payment system है; fees दूसरी companies से कम बताई जाती हैं। Numerical schedule नहीं है; lower fee, zero fee नहीं।
  _screen: Indian origin; lower fees, unquantified._

**b11 · explain · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — RuPay को world's 7th payment gateway कहा गया है। यहाँ gateway शब्द card-payment व्यवस्था के लिए है; technically RuPay card network है। बिना metric के ‘7th’ को size या transaction volume की ranking नहीं मानिए; domestic origin भी domestic-only use नहीं है।
  _screen: Supplied description: world's 7th payment gateway. Technical qualification: RuPay is a card network; no size-ranking metric supplied. Domestic origin ≠ domestic-only use._

**b12 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — Network banks को जोड़ता है; gateway merchant के payment interface से जानकारी processing की ओर भेजता है। Roles अलग हैं।
  _screen: Card network: connects participating banks. Gateway: routes information from merchant interface toward processing._

**b13 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — RuPay domestic है; lower fees का मतलब free नहीं।
  _screen: Comparison recap: similar payment role | Indian domestic origin | lower fees, unquantified. Network ≠ gateway._

### Part c30.3 · classification
_frame: process: Classify RuPay cards by the source of spending funds. · how: Distinguish account money, borrowing and preloaded value. · when: At spending, with repayment later for credit and loading earlier for prepaid. · conditions: The network brand is independent of the funding category. · atmosphere: A bank customer choosing among three card types._

**b14 · question_to_student · step 1: the overview tree first: the basis of division and every member named** — एक जैसे दिखने वाले cards में खर्च का पैसा कहाँ से आता है? Family के 3 रास्ते समझिए: account के पैसे, उधार की limit और पहले भरी value; फिर इनके नाम जोड़ेंगे।
  _screen: Funding tree: linked account | approved borrowing | preloaded value. Reveal card-type names only after each example._

**b15 · example · step 2: every member in the same slots: layer and shape, why it is named so, its special feature, its function with its cause, where it is found by a zoom from the organ, its consequence** — आपके bank account में ₹1,000 हैं; दुकान पर card से ₹200 खर्च किए। Payment के लिए उसी account के पैसे इस्तेमाल हुए, balance ₹800 बचा; पैसे पहले आपके थे, बाद में उधार चुकाना नहीं है।
  _screen: Illustration 1: linked bank account ₹1,000 → ₹800 after ₹200 purchase; no borrowing repayment._

**b15a · explain · step 2: every member in the same slots: layer and shape, why it is named so, its special feature, its function with its cause, where it is found by a zoom from the organ, its consequence** — यह debit card है: linked account से अपने पैसे खर्च हुए, जैसे अपने बटुए से देना।
  _screen: Reveal debit: linked account → purchase; own money supplied before spending._

**b15b · example · step 2: every member in the same slots: layer and shape, why it is named so, its special feature, its function with its cause, where it is found by a zoom from the organ, its consequence** — अब approved borrowing limit ₹1,000 है; उससे ₹200 खर्च किए तो unused limit ₹800 और चुकाने वाला उधार ₹200 हुआ। पैसे अपने deposit से नहीं गए; repayment बाद में करनी है।
  _screen: Illustration 2: borrowing limit ₹1,000 → ₹800 available; ₹200 debt incurred, repay later._

**b15c · explain · step 2: every member in the same slots: layer and shape, why it is named so, its special feature, its function with its cause, where it is found by a zoom from the organ, its consequence** — यह credit card है: approved limit से उधार खर्च, भुगतान बाद में; जैसे उधारी का हिसाब चुकाना।
  _screen: Reveal credit: borrowing facility → purchase → later repayment._

**b15d · example · step 2: every member in the same slots: layer and shape, why it is named so, its special feature, its function with its cause, where it is found by a zoom from the organ, its consequence** — तीसरे card में पहले ₹1,000 value load की; ₹200 खर्च करने पर loaded balance ₹800 बचा। खर्च उसी भरी value से हुआ, जैसे recharge balance; और इस्तेमाल के लिए आगे value फिर load करनी पड़ सकती है।
  _screen: Illustration 3: load ₹1,000 first → spend ₹200 → ₹800 stored value remains; no borrowing created._

**b15e · explain · step 2: every member in the same slots: layer and shape, why it is named so, its special feature, its function with its cause, where it is found by a zoom from the organ, its consequence** — यह prepaid card है: पहले payment करके value भरना, फिर उसी से खर्च करना।
  _screen: Reveal prepaid: load first → spend stored value._

**b16 · compare · step 3: the members compared side by side, the most confused pair named directly** — Debit और prepaid में अपना पैसा है, लेकिन जगह अलग: linked bank account या पहले loaded value। Credit में उधार है; RuPay brand इनमें से किसी एक funding source तक सीमित नहीं।
  _screen: Compare identical slots: funds location | ₹200 spending effect | funding/repayment timing | consequence. Highlight debit versus prepaid._

**b17 · recap · step 4: a memory trick for the family** — याद रखने का तरीका: debit अपना account, credit उधार, prepaid पहले load। ‘RuPay केवल debit है’ इसलिए गलत है।
  _screen: Debit—account; credit—borrow; prepaid—load first._

### Part c31.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define a prepaid electronic voucher restricted to a person and service. · how: Begin with the ₹1,000 vaccination illustration, then unpack the definition. · when: Value is funded before redemption for the intended service. · conditions: Only the intended beneficiary and intended service qualify. · atmosphere: A beneficiary receives an SMS or QR voucher for an authorised vaccination centre._

**b22 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — ₹1,000 की vaccination पक्की करनी हो, cash देना जरूरी है? सरकार आपके account में पैसे डालने के बजाय आपके लिए ₹1,000 voucher SMS या QR Code से भेजती है।
  _screen: Hypothetical vaccination: ₹1,000 voucher, not account credit._

**b23 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — आप authorised centre पर उसी vaccination के लिए redeem करते हैं। पूरी redemption में unused value ₹1,000 से ₹0 होती है और vaccination 0 से 1।
  _screen: Voucher → specified vaccination; ₹1,000 → ₹0 unused._

**b23a · explain · step 1: a concrete example first** — ₹1,000 यहाँ समझाने के लिए लिया amount है, universal limit या किसी actual scheme का दावा नहीं।
  _screen: Hypothetical illustration, not a universal voucher limit or reported scheme amount._

**b24 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — यही 2021 का e-RUPI है: SMS/QR Code based Prepaid Cashless Electronic Voucher। National Payments Corporation of India, NPCI ने UPI platform पर बनाया; prepaid पहले funded, cashless यानी covered payment में cash नहीं देना।
  _screen: e-RUPI • 2021 • NPCI • UPI platform; unpack prepaid and cashless._

**b25 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — Person-specific यानी तय व्यक्ति; purpose-specific यानी तय काम। किसे देना है और किस काम पर इस्तेमाल होगा, अलग checks हैं; एक से दूसरा अपने-आप तय नहीं होता।
  _screen: Two locks: person + purpose._

**b26 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — Unrestricted account credit इसका non-example है; उसमें ये 2 locks नहीं हैं।
  _screen: Boundary test: unrestricted account credit fails the voucher definition._

**b27 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — Definition की पहचान रखिए: prepaid voucher, तय व्यक्ति और तय service।
  _screen: Definition checkpoint: instrument + beneficiary + purpose._

### Part c31.2 · comparison
_frame: process: Distinguish e-RUPI from apps, transfers, currency and unrestricted coupons. · how: Compare digital appearance with the actual function and restrictions. · when: When identifying a phone-delivered payment instrument. · conditions: Digital delivery and UPI infrastructure do not make the voucher general money. · atmosphere: Several different instruments can appear on the same phone._

**b28 · compare · step 1: what is common** — Phone पर app और coupon दोनों दिखें, तो समान हैं? Digital appearance समान है; e-RUPI restrictions वाला gift coupon जैसा है।
  _screen: Common digital form, different functions._

**b29 · compare · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — App interface है; e-RUPI voucher है, bank transfer या digital currency नहीं। Gift coupon की तुलना में भी intended beneficiary और intended service के locks रहेंगे।
  _screen: Voucher versus app, transfer, currency; retain both locks._

**b30 · explain · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — SMS delivery है, currency होने का प्रमाण नहीं; UPI platform भी इसे bank transfer नहीं बनाता।
  _screen: Delivery medium ≠ instrument; underlying platform ≠ bank transfer._

**b31 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — खास trap है e-RUPI बनाम Digital Rupee; gift coupon की analogy भी unrestricted या transferable money नहीं बनाती।
  _screen: Confused pair: restricted voucher ≠ Digital Rupee; do not infer transferability._

**b32 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Comparison में appearance नहीं, काम और restrictions देखकर सही category चुनिए।
  _screen: Difference table: app | bank transfer | currency | restricted voucher._

### Part c31.3 · process
_frame: process: Trace the hypothetical vaccination voucher from government support to redemption. · how: Send ₹1,000 by voucher and redeem it for the specified vaccination. · when: Funding and delivery precede authorised redemption. · conditions: Cash withdrawal and unrelated spending are excluded. · atmosphere: A hypothetical targeted health-service benefit, not a claimed actual scheme._

  _screen: Process audit of the already-shown story: intended outcome = vaccination._

### Part c31.4 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Identify the allowed and excluded uses of the illustrated voucher. · how: Keep purpose and authorised location together. · when: At attempted redemption. · conditions: Vaccination is allowed; cash withdrawal and other purchases are not. · atmosphere: A beneficiary presenting the voucher at an authorised centre._

### Part c32.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define the merchant's card-acceptance fee. · how: Deduct the illustrative ₹20 from a ₹1,000 card sale before naming MDR. · when: For a merchant's card transaction. · conditions: The merchant pays the acquiring bank; the example assumes 2%. · atmosphere: A kirana seller comparing the bill value with the amount received._

**b42 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — किराना दुकान पर card से ₹1,000 दिए; seller को कितने मिलेंगे? Illustrated 2% fee में ₹20 कटेंगे, ₹980 पहुँचेंगे।
  _screen: ₹1,000 sale − ₹20 fee = ₹980 receipt._

**b43 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — यह Merchant Discount Rate, MDR है: seller की हर card transaction पर acquiring bank को दी जाने वाली fee। Acquirer दुकानदार की तरफ payment acceptance संभालता है।
  _screen: MDR: merchant's acceptance fee to acquirer._

**b44 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — Discount customer की price छूट नहीं है। Card acceptance की व्यवस्था की fee से merchant की net receipt कम होती है।
  _screen: Not a price discount; acceptance service fee._

**b45 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — Card sale और bank receipt का फर्क MDR समझाता है। अब इसके recipients देखिए।
  _screen: Gross sale versus net receipt._

### Part c32.2 · structure
_frame: process: Identify participants sharing the merchant-paid fee. · how: Move from merchant-side acquirer to customer-side issuer, network and applicable provider. · when: Within the card-payment acceptance arrangement. · conditions: Provider or gateway participation is conditional; individual shares are unspecified. · atmosphere: Several institutions cooperate behind one card purchase._

**b46 · question_to_student · step 1: the whole in context** — Acquirer को fee दी तो पूरी उसी की? नहीं; payment में कई participants हैं, MDR उनके बीच share होती है।
  _screen: One fee, multiple recipients._

**b47 · explain · step 2: zoom into each part in a fixed path (outside to inside)** — Acquiring Bank यानी दुकान को card से पैसे लेने की व्यवस्था देने वाला bank; Issuing Bank यानी आपको card देने वाला bank। Card Network और जहाँ लागू हो Payment Service Provider/Gateway भी fee share करते हैं।
  _screen: Shop's card-acceptance arrangement → acquirer; your card → issuer; network and applicable provider also share MDR._

**b48 · explain · step 3: function of each part tied to its shape** — Acquirer acceptance संभालता है, issuer card देता है, RuPay जैसे network messages जोड़ते हैं। Gateway/provider processing का interface दे सकता है, जैसे online checkout।
  _screen: Participant roles; networks: RuPay, Visa, Mastercard._

**b49 · explain · step 4: how the parts work together** — मिलकर payment चलाने वाले participants fee बाँटते हैं; collection point पूरी fee नहीं रखता। Individual percentages नहीं दिए हैं।
  _screen: Collection ≠ sole retention; allocations unspecified._

**b50 · recap · step 5: labelled recap** — Acquirer, issuer, network और applicable provider याद रखें। Shared का मतलब equal shares नहीं।
  _screen: Labelled participant recap without allocation percentages._

### Part c32.3 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Identify the direct payer of MDR. · how: Locate the fee deduction on the merchant's receipt rather than the customer's bill. · when: When distinguishing purchase payment from acceptance fee. · conditions: Direct payment obligation is separate from broader pricing decisions. · atmosphere: Customer pays ₹1,000 while seller retains ₹980 in the example._
_skipped steps: 3: This is a payer-identification statement, not a dated statistic.; 5: The full arithmetic is taught in the following figure part._

**b51 · question_to_student · step 1: the fact in one line** — MDR का direct payer कौन? Merchant, customer नहीं; खरीदारी का payment अलग है।
  _screen: MDR payer: merchant._

**b52 · example · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — Customer ₹1,000 देता है, ₹1,020 नहीं। ₹20 merchant की तरफ कटते हैं; उसे ₹980 मिलते हैं।
  _screen: Deduction on merchant side._

**b53 · recap · step 4: what it changes or proves** — Direct payer merchant है। Costs को prices में गिनना अलग economic सवाल है; उससे direct obligation नहीं बदलती।
  _screen: Direct payer ≠ ultimate economic incidence._

### Part c32.4 · figure
_frame: process: Read gross payment, fee distribution and net merchant receipt. · how: Follow the three boxes left to right and calculate 2% of ₹1,000. · when: After identifying the fee and its participants. · conditions: 2% is illustrative; ₹20 is the total fee, not each participant's share. · atmosphere: A simplified card-purchase flow, not a detailed settlement timeline._

**b54 · question_to_student · step 1: what the figure is and why it is here** — ये 3 boxes क्या जोड़ते हैं? Customer का gross payment, merchant की net receipt और participants की fee-sharing।
  _screen: Customer → Merchant → Banks & Payment Network._

**b55 · process_walkthrough · step 2: a reading path across it (labels in order)** — बाएँ Customer से Merchant तक ₹1,000 arrow देखिए, जैसे पूरा bill चुकाना। यह gross purchase payment है, fee नहीं; percentage इसी base पर लगेगा।
  _screen: Trace buyer → ₹1,000 → seller; gross base._

**b56 · process_walkthrough · step 2: a reading path across it (labels in order)** — बीच का Merchant seller है: payment अंदर, MDR shared arrow बाहर। जैसे मिली payment से खर्च अलग रखना, total ₹20 recipients की ओर जाते हैं; ₹980 बचते हैं।
  _screen: Incoming gross; outgoing total fee; retained net._

**b57 · explain · step 2: a reading path across it (labels in order)** — दाएँ grouped box कोई एक organisation नहीं: acquirer, issuer, RuPay/Visa/Mastercard जैसे networks और applicable provider समझिए। Individual shares नहीं दिखाए गए।
  _screen: Expand grouped recipients; no equal-share assumption._

**b58 · explain · step 3: the one thing to remember from it** — 2% rate है: हर ₹100 पर ₹2। इसलिए ₹1,000 × 2 ÷ 100 से ₹20 fee मिलती है, ₹2 नहीं। ₹1,000 से ₹20 घटाइए: net receipt ₹980।
  _screen: ₹1,000 × 2 ÷ 100 = ₹20 fee; ₹1,000 − ₹20 = ₹980 receipt. Distinguish rate from rupees._

**b59 · recap · step 4: link back to the text it explains** — Figure में ₹1,000 gross = ₹980 net + ₹20 total fee। 2% illustrative है, universal rate नहीं।
  _screen: Gross = net + total fee; illustrative rate._

**b60 · recap** — याद रखिए: RuPay network है, e-RUPI restricted voucher है, MDR merchant-paid fee है।
  _screen: Network | restricted voucher | merchant fee._

## v14 · Cross-border payments, polymer notes और cryptocurrency · passed after 4 round(s) · continued from an earlier run

Kind: topic video · budget 6.6-8.8 minutes · spoken about 8.8 minutes
Goal: Learner Project Nexus की जरूरत, leadership, participating systems और country–system matching समझेगा; polymer notes के material–durability claims और cryptocurrency के distributed ownership records को fiat तथा CBDC से अलग करेगा।
Hook: अपने देश में payment तुरंत हो जाता है—दूसरे देश के fast-payment system तक पहुँचने में अलग समस्या क्यों आती है?
Story or analogy: पहले national payment systems को जोड़ने की जरूरत से Nexus का mechanism खुलेगा। फिर स्पष्ट topic transition के साथ 3 अलग बदलाव रखेंगे: payment connectivity, note का material और ownership record की व्यवस्था। Polymer तथा crypto को एक जैसी monetary category नहीं बताएँगे।

**b1 · hook** — Domestic payment तुरंत है; विदेश भेजना मुश्किल क्यों?
  _screen: Connectivity → material → ownership: three different changes._

### Part c33.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define Nexus as a BIS Innovation Hub-led connection between national fast payment systems. · how: Follow a hypothetical India–Singapore remittance through separate domestic payment legs. · when: Explain the intended arrangement, not an asserted live corridor. · conditions: Currency conversion and applicable charges remain necessary. · atmosphere: A sender in India wants to support a relative in Singapore._

**b2 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — Singapore में relative को hypothetical ₹10,000 भेजने हैं। Committed principal ₹0 से ₹10,000 हुआ; UPI Indian हिस्सा संभालेगा।
  _screen: Hypothetical intended design, not live corridor: ₹0 → ₹10,000 principal; UPI._

**b3 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — Connection PayNow तक instruction पहुँचाएगा। Receipt Singapore dollars में होगी; conversion और applicable charges अलग रहेंगे।
  _screen: UPI → intended connection → PayNow; no exchange rate or fees assumed._

**b4 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — यह Project Nexus है; Bank for International Settlements, यानी BIS का Innovation Hub इसे lead करता है।
  _screen: Nexus leadership: BIS Innovation Hub._

**b5 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — Multilateral यानी कई देश; cross-border यानी देशों के बीच payments। यह national fast payment systems, यानी FPS, जोड़ता है।
  _screen: Several countries; separate national FPS connected across borders._

**b6 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — UPI और PayNow अलग रहते हैं; connection उन्हें एक domestic system या एक currency नहीं बनाता।
  _screen: Connect ≠ replace; currencies remain distinct._

**b7 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — Remittance में Nexus याद करो: अलग national FPS साथ काम करते हैं।
  _screen: Separate FPS working together._

### Part c33.3 · cause_effect
_frame: process: Explain intermediary costs and the benefit of shared connection standards. · how: Add an illustrative handling stage, then contrast separate interfaces with a shared design. · when: During remittance routing and payment-system integration. · conditions: Fees and delays vary; connection standardization does not abolish currencies or legal requirements. · atmosphere: A bank transfer counter and a payment-integration desk._

**b16 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — खर्च क्यों बढ़े? Hypothetical ₹10,000 principal, अलग ₹100 fee, 1 working day मानो; outlay ₹10,100 है।
  _screen: Teaching assumptions, not bank quotes: ₹10,000 + ₹100; 1 working day._

**b17 · example · step 2: the cause** — बीच का bank ₹50 और 1 working day जोड़ता है। Fee ₹150, outlay ₹10,150, समय 2 working days हुआ।
  _screen: Principal unchanged at ₹10,000; charges paid separately; no conversion assumed._

**b18 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — ये correspondent banks payment आगे पहुँचाते हैं। Direct arrangement न हो, तो बीच की handling खर्च और समय बढ़ा सकती है।
  _screen: Intermediary handling → possible charges and delay._

**b19 · example · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — Connection-work कैसे घटे? Illustrative 5-system model में India के 4 separate designs को 1 shared design से बदलो।
  _screen: Illustration: 4 partner-specific designs → 1 shared-standard design._

**b20 · example · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — दूसरे hypothetical bank model में ₹1000 instruction भेजना है। 3 partners के अलग interfaces की जगह 1 shared connection लो; यह live corridor का दावा नहीं।
  _screen: Hypothetical bank routing ₹1000: 3 separately designed partner interfaces → 1 standardized connection. Illustration only, not rollout evidence._

**b21 · explain · step 4: the evidence** — यही Nexus का standardized connection है: हर partner का अलग link नहीं बनाना पड़ता; counts illustrative हैं।
  _screen: Shared rules reduce repeated bilateral integration, not actual software counts._

**b22 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — हर transfer की chain समान नहीं; standardization conversion, कानून या responsibilities नहीं मिटाता।
  _screen: Usually ≠ always; cross-border obligations remain._

**b23 · recap · step 6: the chain as recap** — Handling खर्च बढ़ा सकती है; shared standard connection-work घटाता है।
  _screen: Payment friction → need for shared connectivity._

_frame: process: Define polymer notes by their supporting material. · how: Replace cotton-based paper with security-grade BOPP while retaining face value. · when: A hypothetical authorized note is used in a purchase. · conditions: The example does not establish Indian circulation. · atmosphere: A kirana purchase worth ₹10._

  _screen: Cotton-based paper versus security-grade BOPP substrate._

### Part c34.2 · comparison
_frame: process: Distinguish engineered banknotes from ordinary plastic and coexistence from withdrawal. · how: Compare security design and the treatment of existing paper notes. · when: When interpreting a proposed material change. · conditions: A different substrate alone neither creates a banknote nor cancels existing currency. · atmosphere: A shopkeeper checks notes and keeps valid paper notes in use._

**b29 · compare · step 1: what is common** — Plastic और banknote समान हैं? Material-family मिल सकती है; banknote specially engineered होता है।

**b30 · explain · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — Embedded security यानी बनावट में पहचान, जैसे ID की निशानी। Paper notes भी साथ चलेंगे, जैसे wallet में पुराने-नए designs।
  _screen: Built-in authenticity identification; paper and polymer streams remain alongside each other._

**b31 · explain · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — पहचान imitation पकड़ने में मदद करती है। Coexistence दोनों को चलने देता है; withdrawal एक kind हटाता है।
  _screen: Authenticity checks; coexistence versus withdrawal._

**b32 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — Material change को demonetisation मत समझना: existing paper notes बेकार नहीं बताए गए हैं।

**b33 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Engineered note ordinary plastic नहीं; साथ चलना replacement नहीं।

  _screen: 1988 Australia introduction → 2013 Government approval of ₹10 Indian field trial._

### Part c34.5 · cause_effect
_frame: process: Explain durability, anti-counterfeiting and replacement-cost objectives. · how: Reduce illustrative unfit notes, then separate wear resistance from imitation resistance. · when: Over comparable periods of handling and replacement. · conditions: Illustrative quantities are not measured savings; objectives are not achieved outcomes. · atmosphere: A bank cash counter collecting worn notes._

**b45 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — खराब notes कम हों तो क्या बचेगा? Hypothetical equal period में unfit ₹10 notes 100 से 50 मानो।
  _screen: Illustration only: unfit notes 100 → 50._

**b46 · explain · step 2: the cause** — Longer life से collection और replacement घटें: यही durability से replacement costs घटाने का उद्देश्य है।
  _screen: Durability → fewer replacements → lower recurring work._

**b47 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — दूसरा अलग लक्ष्य counterfeiting कठिन करना है: wear रोकना और imitation रोकना अलग काम हैं।
  _screen: Goals: longer life, harder counterfeiting, lower replacement costs._

**b48 · explain · step 4: the evidence** — 100 से 50 illustration है, achieved savings का evidence नहीं।
  _screen: Illustration and stated objectives, not measured results._

**b49 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — Unit production सस्ता होना जरूरी नहीं; कम replacement बचत दे सकता है। Durable भी counterfeit-proof नहीं।
  _screen: Production cost ≠ replacement cost; durable ≠ counterfeit-proof._

**b50 · recap · step 6: the chain as recap** — Durability replacement घटाए; security imitation रोके।
  _screen: Two separate benefit chains; objectives, not guaranteed outcomes._

### Part c35.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define cryptocurrency through a blockchain-recorded digital transfer. · how: Track changes in hypothetical holdings and explain linked records. · when: After a transfer is validated. · conditions: Fees are ignored in the illustration; digital form does not establish official monetary status. · atmosphere: Two users transferring a hypothetical digital asset._

**b51 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — बिना note ownership कैसे बदले? Hypothetically 1 unit भेजकर validate होने पर linked blocks में record बना। Fees छोड़कर sender 2 से 1, receiver 0 से 1 हुआ।
  _screen: Hypothetical transfer; fees ignored: authorize 1 → validate → linked-block record; sender 2 → 1, recipient 0 → 1._

**b52 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — यह cryptocurrency है: blockchain से created और stored digital/virtual currency। Blockchain जुड़े blocks का record है, जैसे जुड़े हिसाब-पन्ने।
  _screen: Blockchain: linked blocks containing transaction records._

**b53 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — हर phone balance crypto नहीं; wallet आम तौर पर authorization keys संभालता है, literal coins नहीं।
  _screen: Keys in wallet; transaction and ownership-related records on blockchain._

**b54 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — Crypto का blockchain record समझा; अब official status देखें।
  _screen: Blockchain records ≠ official monetary status._

### Part c35.2 · comparison
_frame: process: Separate privately created crypto from RBI-issued digital money. · how: Compare issuer and legal-tender status rather than digital appearance. · when: When classifying an asset as official money or a private asset. · conditions: Absence of legal-tender status alone does not determine legality of holding or trading. · atmosphere: Two digital balances with different institutional status._

**b55 · compare · step 1: what is common** — Phone पर crypto और Digital Rupee समान लगें? Digital रूप समान, issuer और legal status अलग हैं।
  _screen: Cryptocurrency | Digital Rupee: both digital; compare issuer and legal status next._

**b56 · compare · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — Crypto private asset है, RBI-issued नहीं; Digital Rupee RBI-issued CBDC है। India में पहला legal tender नहीं, दूसरा है।
  _screen: Cryptocurrency | Digital Rupee. Issuer: private, not RBI | RBI. Indian legal tender: no | yes._

**b57 · explain · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — Digital रूप है; legal tender payment का legal status है। Transfer होना यह status नहीं देता।
  _screen: Digital form and transferability do not confer legal-tender status._

**b58 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — Not legal tender सीधे illegal नहीं है; holding, trading और taxation अलग legal सवाल हैं।
  _screen: Separate legal questions._

**b59 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Crypto private, legal tender नहीं; Digital Rupee RBI का, legal tender है।
  _screen: Difference table: Cryptocurrency | Digital Rupee. Digital: yes | yes. Issuer: private, not RBI | RBI. Indian legal tender: no | yes._

### Part c35.3 · process
_frame: process: Explain ownership recording through a distributed ledger. · how: Validate a proposed transfer and update records maintained across participating computers. · when: A valid transfer is accepted under network rules. · conditions: An unaccepted instruction does not establish changed ownership; network details vary. · atmosphere: Several computers maintain a consistent ownership record._

**b60 · example · step 1: the result first (what it makes)** — Central bank-copy बिना ownership कैसे मिले? Hypothetical transfer का result sender 3 से 2, receiver 0 से 1 होना चाहिए।
  _screen: Desired result; fees ignored: sender 3 → 2, recipient 0 → 1._

**b61 · process_walkthrough · step 2: inputs and the place** — Sender 1 unit authorize करता है; participating computers network rules से instruction जाँचते हैं।
  _screen: Input: authorized transfer; place: participating network computers._

**b62 · explain · step 3: each step with its cause and its condition** — Valid transfer accept हो तो distributed ledger बदले: कई computers की हिसाब-कॉपियों में मिलती entry, bank या government की अकेली copy नहीं।
  _screen: Network rules validate and accept the transfer; matching ownership entries update across participating computers._

**b63 · misconception · step 4: what breaks when a step fails** — Update न हो तो वही unit पुराने owner के पास उपलब्ध दिख सकती है।
  _screen: Missing update risks inconsistent ownership and repeated spending._

**b64 · process_walkthrough · step 5: the whole run once more at speed** — जाँचकर accept किया; holdings 2 और 1 हुईं।
  _screen: Replay: authorize → validate → accept → sender 2, recipient 1._

**b65 · recap · step 6: recap as a flow** — Authorize, validate, update: भरोसा network rules पर है।
  _screen: Authorize → validate → distributed ownership update._

### Part c35.4 · cause_effect
_frame: process: Explain distrust and payment grievances as motivations for alternatives. · how: Trace mortgage default to loss, and a merchant fee to lower net receipts. · when: During financial distress or costly and incompatible payment experiences. · conditions: Motivation proves neither crypto safety nor freedom from fees; crisis losses do not prove dollar purchasing-power erosion. · atmosphere: A hypothetical US mortgage lender and an Indian kirana payment counter._

**b66 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — Banking पर भरोसा क्यों हिले? Hypothetical weak-credit borrower पर $100000 बाकी हैं; monthly payment $800 से $0 हुई।
  _screen: Hypothetical US mortgage, not historical statistics._

**b67 · example · step 2: the cause** — घर से net $70000 मिले: $30000 shortfall हुआ। कई loans और mortgage-backed claims के losses institutions कमजोर कर सकते हैं।
  _screen: Other recoveries ignored: $100000 − $70000 = $30000 loss._

**b68 · example · step 2: the cause** — Charges से परेशानी क्यों? Hypothetical ₹100 card sale में ₹1 fee कटे; merchant को ₹99 मिले।
  _screen: Illustrative fee, not actual tariff: ₹100 → ₹99 proceeds._

**b69 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — यह subprime distress है: losses से distrust, फिर alternatives में interest हो सकता है। Anarchist groups central authority का विरोध करते हैं।
  _screen: Loan losses → financial disruption → possible distrust → interest in alternatives._

**b70 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — MDR, यानी Merchant Discount Rate, और e-banking/card charges भी motives हैं। Interoperability यानी systems साथ काम करें; incompatibility payment रोक सकती है।
  _screen: Illustrative merchant fee lowers receipts. MDR: Merchant Discount Rate. Interoperability: different systems working together._

**b71 · explain · step 4: the evidence** — कुछ anarchist groups ने USA की 2007 Subprime Crisis से dollar की purchasing power घटने को FIAT MONEY पर distrust का कारण माना। यह दावा है, losses से पक्का नतीजा नहीं।
  _screen: Attributed claim: crisis → dollar purchasing-power erosion → fiat distrust. Loan losses do not establish that purchasing-power claim._

**b72 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — FIAT MONEY monetary/legal system से चलता है, fixed-gold conversion का वादा नहीं। Purchasing power यानी रकम कितना सामान खरीदे; loan losses इसका घटना साबित नहीं करते।
  _screen: Fiat: monetary/legal backing, not fixed commodity conversion. Purchasing power: goods and services a sum buys. Financial losses ≠ proof of purchasing-power erosion._

**b73 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — इससे crypto safe या free साबित नहीं होता; उसकी fees और compatibility limits भी हैं।
  _screen: Motivation does not prove safety, zero fees or universal interoperability._

**b74 · recap · step 6: the chain as recap** — Distrust और charges alternatives की चाह समझाते हैं, सफलता नहीं।
  _screen: Distrust and payment grievances motivate alternatives; they do not establish benefits._

**b75 · recap** — Connectivity, material, private records: तीनों अलग रखो।
  _screen: Nexus: payment connectivity | Polymer: note material | Crypto: private ownership records. Next: money supply._

## v15 · Money supply: M0 से M4 तक क्या गिना जाता है? · passed after 4 round(s) · reused

Kind: topic video · budget 9.0-12.0 minutes · spoken about 12.0 minutes
Goal: Learner aggregate overview से M0–M4 की पूरी family समझेगा; formulas को component by component बनाएगा और currency in circulation, public currency, RBI deposits, demand deposits, time deposits तथा post-office coverage की boundaries पहचान सकेगा।
Hook: जेब का cash, bank balance और fixed deposit—देश का money गिनते समय क्या सबको हर बार एक ही total में जोड़ेंगे?
Story or analogy: पहले अलग जगह रखी रकम की situation, फिर मापने की जरूरत और aggregate names आएँगे। Overview के बाद M0 का base अलग समझाएँगे; M1–M4 को component cards से बनाएँगे। M1–M2 और M3–M4 की direct comparison, liquidity, NSC exclusion और inclusion–exclusion checks होंगे; M0 को बाकी aggregates की सीधी nested सीढ़ी नहीं दिखाएँगे।

**b1 · hook** — Wallet का cash, bank balance और FD दिख रहे हैं? Money गिनते समय क्या हमेशा सब जोड़ेंगे?
  _screen: Wallet, bank balance and FD; question mark over total._

### Part c36.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define the economy's money holdings at an instant. · how: Transfer ₹100 between two holders, then distinguish the unchanged stock from payments. · when: Measure at one selected instant, not across a month. · conditions: Specify eligible monetary components and avoid repeated counting. · atmosphere: A buyer pays a neighbourhood kirana shop._

**b2 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — खरीदार के ₹100, दुकानदार के ₹0 हैं। खरीदारी के बाद दोनों के balances उलट गए, लेकिन combined cash ₹100 ही रहा।
  _screen: Buyer ₹100 → ₹0; shopkeeper ₹0 → ₹100; total unchanged._

**b3 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — Money supply economy में एक तय पल पर spending, saving और payments के लिए उपलब्ध money है। Holdings की photo सोचो; यह stock है।
  _screen: Freeze economy-wide holdings at one instant._

**b4 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — महीने के payments का total यह stock नहीं है। वही ₹100 बार-बार इस्तेमाल हो सकता है।
  _screen: Repeated payments with one ₹100 note versus one snapshot._

**b5 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — यानी तय पल की holdings गिनेंगे। Cash और deposits इस्तेमाल करने की आसानी अलग है, इसलिए अलग measures चाहिए।
  _screen: Snapshot plus component boundary._

### Part c36.2 · classification
_frame: process: Organize the five aggregate names by coverage. · how: Keep M0 separate; branch M2 and M3 from M1 and extend M3 to M4. · when: Overview precedes detailed formulas. · conditions: Overlapping aggregates must not be added together. · atmosphere: A household and shop hold cash and several kinds of deposits._

**b6 · example · step 1: the overview tree first: the basis of division and every member named** — अलग holdings कैसे गिनोगे? Hypothetical cash ₹1000, demand account ₹4000, bank में तय समय की रकम ₹6000, Post Office savings ₹2000 और दूसरे eligible deposits ₹3000 लो; RBI के other deposits zero मानो।
  _screen: Hypothetical holdings cards; separate excluded NSC ₹5000 card. Cash plus demand ₹5000; add PO savings ₹7000; alternatively add bank term holdings ₹11000; add qualifying PO total ₹16000. Do not yet label totals with aggregate names._

**b7 · explain · step 2: every member in the same slots: layer and shape, why it is named so, its special feature, its function with its cause, where it is found by a zoom from the organ, its consequence** — Cash और demand ₹5000 हैं; Post Office savings के साथ ₹7000। ₹5000 में bank की ₹6000 जोड़ो: ₹11000; फिर Post Office की ₹5000 जोड़ो: ₹16000। ये M1, M2, M3, M4 हैं; M0 अलग reserve base है।
  _screen: Reveal M1 ₹5000, M2 ₹7000, M3 ₹11000 and M4 ₹16000 after calculations. M0 separate; M2 and M3 branch from M1. NSC excluded._

**b8 · compare · step 3: the members compared side by side, the most confused pair named directly** — अब demand से ₹1000 तय समय की deposit में रखो: balances ₹3000 और ₹7000 हुए। M1 ₹4000, M2 ₹6000 हुए; M3 ₹11000 और M4 ₹16000 रहे। इसलिए M2 और M3 की additions अलग समझो।
  _screen: Transfer ₹1000 between demand and term columns. Before/after four-total table; no new money created._

**b9 · recap · step 4: a memory trick for the family** — याद रखो: M0 base; M2 Post Office savings, M3 bank time जोड़ता है। M3 important reporting measure, M4 broadest है। Overlapping totals मत जोड़ना।
  _screen: Memory branches from M1; M0 separate. M3 importance versus M4 breadth. Cross out sum of aggregates._

_frame: process: Read and qualify the five-tier money pyramid. · how: Scan apex to base, then replace misleading cumulative implications with component baskets. · when: After the family overview and before detailed mechanisms. · conditions: Printed abbreviations are not complete definitions. · atmosphere: Five widening tiers beside an accurate branch diagram._

**b10 · question_to_student · step 1: what the figure is and why it is here** — Pyramid नीचे चौड़ा होता दिख रहा है? इसकी 5 tiers M0 से M4 और deposit categories दिखाती हैं।

**b14 · misconception · step 3: the one thing to remember from it** — Plus देखकर पिछली tier मत जोड़ना: M0 अलग है, M3 का base M1 है। पूरी M1 formula में OD भी है।
  _screen: Correct branches beside pyramid; OD added to full M1 definition._

**b15 · recap · step 4: link back to the text it explains** — Picture से labels पहचानो, formulas से calculation करो। M2 सिर्फ Post Office savings जोड़ता है; M4 qualifying total, NSC नहीं।
  _screen: Diagram for orientation; component baskets for calculation._

### Part c36.4 · process
_frame: process: Money moves through payments and deposits. · how: Follow a household payment to a shop and its bank, with a separate bank–RBI link. · when: During purchases, wages, deposits and lending. · conditions: A payment generally transfers money; every household payment need not directly involve RBI. · atmosphere: A local shop connected to the banking system._

**b16 · example · step 1: the result first (what it makes)** — किराने पर दिए ₹500 कहाँ गए? दुकानदार की sale receipt ₹0 से ₹500 हुई; उसने पैसे bank में जमा किए।
  _screen: ₹500 household payment followed by shop deposit._

**b17 · explain · step 2: inputs and the place** — यह money circulation है, यानी दुकान पर दिया पैसा आगे चलता है। Households, businesses और banks जुड़े हैं; banks का RBI से connection है।
  _screen: Follow the shop payment into its bank; show a separate bank–RBI connection._

**b18 · process_walkthrough · step 3: each step with its cause and its condition** — Purchases, wages, deposits और loans पर पैसे चलते हैं। RBI transactions banks को जोड़ते हैं; हर खरीदारी सीधे RBI से नहीं गुजरती।
  _screen: Animate payment paths and separate RBI transactions._

**b19 · misconception · step 4: what breaks when a step fails** — Recipient अगला payment रोक दे तो आगे movement रुकेगा। पैसा गायब नहीं हुआ; अभी उसके पास है।
  _screen: Pause next payment; balance stays with recipient._

**b20 · process_walkthrough · step 5: the whole run once more at speed** — फिर चलाओ: खरीदार ने दिया, दुकानदार ने bank में जमा किया। Holder और form बदले।
  _screen: Replay purchase and bank deposit._

**b21 · recap · step 6: recap as a flow** — Circulation movement है; Money supply तय पल की holdings है। पैसा चलना हमेशा नया पैसा बनना नहीं है।
  _screen: Flow versus stock._

### Part c37.1 · definition
_frame: process: Identify directly RBI-created reserve money. · how: An RBI security purchase credits a bank's reserve account. · when: Central-bank money provides a base for subsequent banking activity. · conditions: Reserves support settlement and potential lending, not compulsory lending. · atmosphere: RBI and a commercial bank's reserve account._

**b22 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — Bank का RBI balance कैसे बढ़े? RBI ₹100 की security खरीदकर credit करे, तो उसका balance ₹100 से ₹200 होगा।
  _screen: Hypothetical purchase; RBI account ₹100 → ₹200._

**b23 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — सीधे RBI-created base M0 है: Reserve Money या High-Powered Money। Building के foundation जैसा, यह आगे bank money बनने का आधार है।
  _screen: M0 foundation beneath bank deposits._

**b24 · misconception · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — तुम्हारा deposit M0 नहीं। Bank का RBI balance अलग है; यह withdrawals और interbank payments सँभालता है।
  _screen: Customer deposit versus bank's RBI balance._

**b25 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — M0 base है, public deposits नहीं; lending को support करता है, मजबूर नहीं करता।
  _screen: Reserve base supports conditional lending._

### Part c37.2 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Construct M0 from three monetary stocks. · how: Distinguish circulating currency, other RBI deposits and bankers' RBI deposits. · when: Use balances for the same instant. · conditions: Use common units; include bank vault cash and CRR balances without double counting. · atmosphere: Public wallets, a bank vault and accounts held at RBI._

**b26 · example · step 1: the fact in one line** — Public cash ₹1000, vault cash ₹200 और bank का RBI balance ₹800 हो तो कितना base पैसा है? Other RBI deposits zero मानकर total ₹2000 है।
  _screen: Hypothetical monetary-base holdings: ₹1000 + ₹200 + ₹800 = ₹2000; no formula labels yet._

**b27 · example · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — Bank RBI account से ₹100 के notes लेता है। Vault cash ₹300 और RBI balance ₹700 हो जाता है। M0 ₹2000 रहता है; public cash नहीं बढ़ता।
  _screen: Currency in circulation ₹1200 → ₹1300; bankers' RBI deposits ₹800 → ₹700; M0 unchanged at ₹2000._

**b28 · explain · step 3: where it comes from (book or outside, dated)** — C यानी Currency in circulation में public और bank cash हैं। Bankers' Deposits में CRR balances भी हैं; Cash Reserve Ratio relevant bank liabilities का RBI में रखा जाने वाला तय हिस्सा है।
  _screen: C spans wallets and vault. Bankers' Deposits with RBI includes CRR; no numerical ratio supplied._

**b29 · example · step 4: what it changes or proves** — OD का standard outside example IMF का RBI deposit है। Commercial bank का RBI account Bankers' Deposits है; तुम्हारा commercial-bank account दोनों नहीं। हर दूसरा RBI balance अपने-आप OD नहीं होता।
  _screen: Label outside example: IMF = International Monetary Fund. Compare holder, account institution and category in three rows; household account is a commercial-bank liability, not an RBI liability._

**b30 · recap · step 5: a formula built term by term when there is one** — अब formula बनाओ: M0 = C + OD + Bankers' Deposits। तीनों same-date stocks, एक unit में जोड़ो; customer deposits इसमें मत डालना।
  _screen: Build formula from the three explained boxes; common date and monetary unit._

### Part c37.3 · cause_effect
_frame: process: Bank lending creates deposit money across the banking system. · how: An ₹80 loan and a subsequent ₹60 loan create ₹140 of deposits under stated assumptions. · when: Lending and interbank settlement occur in successive rounds. · conditions: Reserves, capital, liquidity, lending standards and credit demand constrain expansion. · atmosphere: Two banks, a business borrower and a supplier._

**b31 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — Loan से balance कैसे बढ़ता है? सारी ज़रूरी शर्तें पूरी मानो: Bank A ₹80 loan deposit बनाता है; borrower Bank B के supplier को देता है।
  _screen: ₹80 loan creates deposit; supplier receives payment at Bank B._

**b32 · example · step 2: the cause** — Bank B शर्तें पूरी करके ₹60 loan देता है। Repayments और cash leakage छोड़ें तो इन loans से नए deposits ₹0 से ₹140 हुए।
  _screen: Second loan ₹60; illustrated new deposits total ₹140._

**b33 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — यह reserves-supported multiple expansion है। Loan deposit बनाता है; banks के बीच payment settle करने के लिए reserve base चाहिए।
  _screen: Reserve support → lending → deposit → settlement._

**b34 · explain · step 4: the evidence** — Account में loan credit देखो। Recipient पैसे bank में रखे तो deposits system में रहते हैं।
  _screen: Loan credit followed by recipient's bank balance._

**b35 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — Expansion guaranteed नहीं: withdrawals, reserve needs, capital, lending standards और loan demand इसे रोक सकते हैं। Bank बिना सीमा lending नहीं कर सकता।
  _screen: Constraints gate further lending._

**b36 · recap · step 6: the chain as recap** — Reserve support से loan deposit, settlement और conditional lending होती है। Expansion पूरे banking system में है।
  _screen: Reserve support → loan deposit → settlement → conditional further lending._

### Part c38.1 · definition
_frame: process: Define M1 through near-immediate spendability. · how: Demonstrate a cash purchase and a demand-account payment before explaining Narrow Money. · when: Funds are needed for everyday transactions. · conditions: Almost immediate use does not mean every form of savings qualifies. · atmosphere: A customer paying for groceries._

**b37 · example · step 1: a concrete example first** — ₹200 का किराना अभी चाहिए? ₹500 cash से दोगे तो ₹300 बचेंगे; ₹1000 payment-ready account से ₹300 दो तो ₹700 बचेंगे।
  _screen: Two separate immediate-payment examples._

**b38 · explain · step 2: the book's definition word by word** — यह immediate-payment focus M1, Narrow Money की पहचान है। यह public का सबसे liquid money है, यानी जल्दी इस्तेमाल होने वाला पैसा।
  _screen: M1: most liquid; almost immediately spendable._

**b39 · compare · step 3: a non-example that tests the boundary** — FD में access की शर्तें हो सकती हैं। Narrow तुरंत इस्तेमाल वाले components हैं, पतले notes नहीं।
  _screen: FD outside immediate-payment boundary._

**b40 · recap · step 4: where the term is used next in the chapter** — M1 almost immediately spendable components गिनता है। अब formula बनाओ।
  _screen: Payment-ready components become formula cards._

### Part c38.2 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Build M1 and M2 component by component. · how: Add public currency, demand deposits and other RBI deposits; then add eligible post-office savings deposits. · when: Compare updated balances after completed transfers. · conditions: Exclude bank vault cash from public currency and NSC from the M2 addition. · atmosphere: Wallet, bank account and post-office passbook._

**b41 · example · step 1: the fact in one line** — Cash ₹1000, demand balance ₹4000 और other RBI deposits zero हों तो ₹5000 हुए। Post Office savings ₹2000 भी गिनें तो ₹7000 होंगे; किस boundary में क्या आएगा?
  _screen: Hypothetical contribution, not national totals. Calculate ₹5000 and ₹7000 before revealing aggregate labels._

**b42 · example · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — ₹500 cash Post Office savings में रखो: cash ₹500, savings ₹2500 हुए। ₹4500 + ₹2500 फिर ₹7000 हैं। इस wider total को M2, Intermediate Money कहते हैं; इसका base M1 है।
  _screen: Completed hypothetical transfer, no offsetting changes. M1 contribution ₹5000 → ₹4500; M2 ₹7000 unchanged. Reveal M2 name after arithmetic._

**b43 · explain · step 3: where it comes from (book or outside, dated)** — Demand deposits में on-demand current और savings balances आते हैं। M1 में bank vault cash और Bankers' Deposits नहीं; OD दोनों formulas में है।
  _screen: M0 versus M1 inclusion table._

**b44 · example · step 4: what it changes or proves** — Original balances पर लौटो। Bank से ₹500 निकालो तो cash ₹1500, demand ₹3500: M1 ₹5000 रहा। Account की पुरानी रकम फिर जोड़ोगे तो double counting होगी।
  _screen: Reset original cash ₹1000 and demand ₹4000; withdraw ₹500; sum updated balances only._

**b45 · recap · step 5: a formula built term by term when there is one** — M1 = public currency + Demand Deposits + OD। M2 = M1 + Post Office savings deposits; bank time deposits और NSC बाहर हैं।
  _screen: Build both formulas; excluded cards remain outside._

### Part c38.3 · classification
_frame: process: Classify post-office products as included or excluded in the M2 addition. · how: Separate savings-bank deposits from NSC and similar instruments. · when: Before adding post-office holdings to M1. · conditions: Product type, not the institution alone, determines inclusion. · atmosphere: Two different savings products offered at a post office._

**b46 · question_to_student · step 1: the overview tree first: the basis of division and every member named** — Post Office passbook और certificate दोनों जोड़ेंगे? M2 addition में savings-bank deposits included हैं; NSC और similar instruments excluded हैं।
  _screen: Two-branch product classification._

**b47 · explain · step 2: every member in the same slots: layer and shape, why it is named so, its special feature, its function with its cause, where it is found by a zoom from the organ, its consequence** — Savings deposit account balance है, इसलिए इस addition में आता है। NSC time-bound certificate है; institution वही हो, product अलग है।
  _screen: Parallel product slots: form, access, institution, inclusion._

**b48 · compare · step 3: the members compared side by side, the most confused pair named directly** — Deposit और certificate अलग हैं; Post Office नाम देखकर दोनों मत जोड़ना।
  _screen: Deposit included; certificate excluded._

**b49 · recap · step 4: a memory trick for the family** — Exam trick: M2 में eligible savings deposit है, हर savings product नहीं।
  _screen: Read product type, not merely institution._

### Part c38.4 · comparison
_frame: process: Compare liquidity and convenience of savings deposits. · how: Contrast availability of funds with ease of accessing or using them. · when: A saver needs money for a payment. · conditions: Relative inconvenience does not imply inaccessibility; no current restrictions are assumed. · atmosphere: A saver comparing bank and post-office account access._

**b50 · question_to_student · step 1: what is common** — दो accounts में ₹1000 हों तो ₹300 का bill देना बराबर आसान होगा? दोनों में रकम है; अब इस्तेमाल का रास्ता देखो।
  _screen: Hypothetical equal balances: ordinary bank deposit ₹1000 and PO savings ₹1000; grocery bill ₹300._

**b51 · compare · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — मानो bank account से bill सीधे दे सकते हो, लेकिन चुने गए Post Office access में पहले cash निकालना पड़े। पैसे दोनों जगह हैं; दूसरे रास्ते में 1 extra step है।
  _screen: Illustration only: bank account → ₹300 payment; selected PO access → cash withdrawal → ₹300 payment. Not a universal current restriction._

**b52 · explain · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — यह hypothetical है, universal restriction नहीं। रकम निकाल सकना liquidity है; payment के steps convenience बदलते हैं।
  _screen: Hypothetical access paths; availability versus access steps._

**b53 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — Fairly liquid और slightly less convenient साथ हो सकते हैं। Less convenient का मतलब inaccessible नहीं।
  _screen: Less convenient ≠ inaccessible._

**b54 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Post Office savings fairly liquid हैं, ordinary bank deposits से थोड़ी कम convenient; availability और access अलग हैं।
  _screen: Comparison table: liquidity and relative convenience._

### Part c39.1 · facts_numbers
_frame: process: Construct M3 and M4 and distinguish importance from breadth. · how: Add bank time deposits to M1, then qualifying post-office deposits to M3. · when: Use same-date stocks; reset assumptions for separate worked examples. · conditions: M3 does not start from M2; M4 excludes NSC and counts post-office savings once. · atmosphere: A bank FD conversion, a monetary-statistics desk and a news headline._

**b55 · example · step 1: the fact in one line** — Account से ₹1000 agreed date तक अलग रखो तो payment-ready रकम क्या होगी? जल्दी निकालने पर शर्तें हो सकती हैं। ऐसी bank रकम Time Deposits है; FD और recurring deposits इसके examples हैं।
  _screen: Demand account transfers ₹1000 into an agreed-term holding. Conditional premature access; reveal Time Deposits, FD and recurring deposits after the situation._

**b56 · explain · step 1: the fact in one line** — Broadest coverage, reporting importance नहीं है। Broadest label अकेला component formula नहीं देता।
  _screen: Breadth label versus component formula and reporting role._

**b57 · example · step 2: the number shown to scale against something familiar** — M1 ₹5000 में bank time deposits ₹6000 जोड़ो: ₹11000, यानी M3 या Broad Money। फिर Post Office savings ₹2000 और दूसरे eligible deposits ₹3000 जोड़ो: ₹16000, यानी M4; NSC बाहर है।
  _screen: Hypothetical contributions, OD zero. Calculate before revealing M3 and M4 labels; NSC card excluded. PO savings counted once._

**b58 · explain · step 3: where it comes from (book or outside, dated)** — M3 important money-supply measure है; news में growth आम तौर पर इसी की होती है। M4 broadest है। Recurring deposits यानी नियमित किस्तों की बचत भी bank time deposits में आती है।
  _screen: Mock M3 growth headline; M3 importance versus M4 breadth; FD, RD and other bank time deposits._

**b59 · example · step 4: what it changes or proves** — ₹1000 demand से FD में करो: demand ₹3000, time ₹7000। M1 ₹4000 हुआ; M3 ₹11000 रहा, क्योंकि दोनों components उसमें हैं।
  _screen: Reset original hypothetical holdings. Demand ₹4000 → ₹3000; time ₹6000 → ₹7000; M3 unchanged._

**b60 · example · step 4: what it changes or proves** — अलग snapshots में M3 ₹1000 crore स्थिर, eligible Post Office deposits ₹200 से ₹250 crore हों: M4 ₹1200 से ₹1250 crore होगा।
  _screen: Same-date stocks per snapshot; common crore-rupee unit; NSC excluded._

**b61 · misconception · step 4: what it changes or proves** — यह assumed accounting comparison है, transfer से money creation नहीं। Broadest coverage, important reporting और जमीन समेत total wealth अलग बातें हैं।
  _screen: Breadth ≠ importance ≠ total wealth._

**b62 · recap · step 5: a formula built term by term when there is one** — M3 = M1 + bank time deposits, M2 नहीं। M4 = M3 + all qualifying Post Office deposits, excluding NSC; savings दोबारा मत जोड़ना।
  _screen: Build formulas term by term; count eligible savings once._

### Part c39.2 · cause_effect
_frame: process: Explain RBI monitoring and the NSC exclusion. · how: Trace loan-created deposits into monetary indicators, then contrast spendable cash with a long-term certificate. · when: Policy analysis compares monetary growth with credit, output and prices. · conditions: M3 is an indicator, not proof of inflation; specific exclusions do not exclude all term deposits. · atmosphere: A local business loan and a family's long-term savings decision._

**b63 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — नया loan क्या बदलता है? ₹100 loan deposit बना, supplier ने bank में रखा; बाकी बदलाव न हों तो M3 ₹1000 से ₹1100 हुआ।
  _screen: New lending increases illustrative M3 by ₹100._

**b64 · example · step 1: the effect students can see** — Certificate से किराना दोगे? ₹2000 cash में से ₹1000 का NSC खरीदो: cash ₹1000 बचेगा; खरीदारी में वही इस्तेमाल होगा, certificate नहीं।
  _screen: Cash ₹2000 → ₹1000; NSC ₹0 → ₹1000._

**b65 · explain · step 2: the cause** — पहला example RBI की M3 monitoring समझाता है। दूसरा NSC exclusion: वह long-term savings instrument है, readily spendable money नहीं।
  _screen: Two reasoning tracks: monitoring and exclusion._

**b66 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — M3 से liquidity और credit growth के संकेत मिलते हैं। यानी account balances और lending साथ जाँचेंगे; खरीदने की ताकत सामान से तेज़ बढ़े तो दामों पर दबाव बन सकता है।
  _screen: Compare account balances and lending; then compare spending power with available goods, using a conditional price-pressure arrow._

**b67 · explain · step 3: each link of the chain with its mechanism** — Policy effectiveness का check है: RBI के कदमों के बाद money, credit और prices चाही दिशा में गए? NSC लंबे समय की saving है, आज का payment balance नहीं।
  _screen: Policy dashboard checks intended direction; separate long-term certificate from today's payment balance._

**b68 · explain · step 4: the evidence** — Credit और prices भी जाँचो; NSC बाहर रखो।
  _screen: Read M3 alongside credit and price data; a Post Office label does not make NSC eligible._

**b69 · misconception · step 5: the exception or limit** — M3 बढ़ना inflation का proof नहीं; यह loans का total भी नहीं। NSC बाहर होने से सारे long-term deposits बाहर नहीं होते।
  _screen: Reject deterministic inflation and blanket long-term-deposit exclusion._

**b70 · recap · step 6: the chain as recap** — RBI के checks हैं inflationary pressures, liquidity, credit growth और policy effectiveness। NSC long-term, readily spendable नहीं; इसलिए M4 से बाहर है।
  _screen: Four monitoring gauges; NSC exclusion chain._

**b71 · recap** — आखिरी check: किसका balance, कहाँ, कौन-सा product? इसी से सही aggregate चुनोगे।
  _screen: Final holder–institution–product checklist beside the aggregate map: M0 separate; M2 and M3 branch from M1._

## v16 · PYQ अभ्यास: blockchain, RTGS–NEFT और BHIM · passed after 4 round(s) · reused

Kind: topic video · budget 4.8-6.5 minutes · spoken about 6.4 minutes
Goal: Learner supplied questions के हर statement और option का कारण सहित निर्णय करेगा; blockchain applications और permission, settlement–charges–hours तथा BHIM interoperability और authentication की traps अलग पहचान सकेगा।
Hook: किसी statement का पहला हिस्सा सही हो, लेकिन आखिरी शब्द गलत हो—क्या आप उसे फिर भी सही चुनेंगे?
Story or analogy: हर supplied PYQ में पहले learner का निर्णय, फिर statement को छोटे claims में तोड़कर mechanism से जाँच होगी। Blockchain का ledger structure और applications जोड़ेंगे; technology को cryptocurrency तक सीमित नहीं करेंगे। Pre'25 wording में charge direction और hours, तथा BHIM में authentication factors और transaction steps अलग जाँचेंगे।

**b1 · hook** — आधा statement सही हो, तो पूरा चुनोगे?
  _screen: आधा सही ≠ पूरा सही | 2020 • 2025 • 2018_

### Part c40.1 · structure
_frame: process: Separate public inspection from unilateral control of a shared ledger. · how: Move from the shared record to network copies and validation rules. · when: Reading is possible independently of proposing and accepting updates. · conditions: The question assumes a public blockchain; restricted networks need qualification. · atmosphere: A hypothetical mandi with buyer and seller inspecting matching delivery records._

**b2 · example · step 1: the whole in context** — मंडी का हिसाब कई लोगों के पास हो, तो क्या एक का बदलाव सबका हिसाब बदल देगा? Public blockchain का ledger ऐसा साझा electronic हिसाब है।
  _screen: Hypothetical mandi → shared electronic ledger_

**b3 · explain · step 2: zoom into each part in a fixed path (outside to inside)** — Entry के पीछे participating computers पर copies हैं। बदलाव के लिए network के validation और agreement rules पूरे होने चाहिए।
  _screen: Entry → network copies → validation rules_

**b4 · explain · step 3: function of each part tied to its shape** — साझा rules एक user को अकेले history बदलने से रोकते हैं। देख सकना, बदलने का अधिकार नहीं है।
  _screen: Public inspection ≠ unilateral editing_

**b5 · explain · step 4: how the parts work together** — Entry पहले proposal है; checks के बाद accepted history बनती है। अपनी copy बदलना, network बदलना नहीं है।
  _screen: Proposal → checks → accepted shared history_

**b6 · recap · step 5: labelled recap** — हिसाब साझा है; control अकेले का नहीं।
  _screen: Ledger | network copies | rule-based acceptance_

### Part c40.2 · process
_frame: process: Accept an authorised delivery record without limiting the technology to cryptocurrency. · how: Submit, validate, agree and replicate; reject unsupported overwrites. · when: The entry becomes accepted only after required checks and agreement. · conditions: Application authorisation and reliable reporting remain necessary; open development does not waive law. · atmosphere: A hypothetical public-network onion delivery application._

**b7 · example · step 1: the result first (what it makes)** — क्या इसमें प्याज की delivery लिख सकते हैं? Hypothetical मंडी में recorded quantity 0 से 10 sacks करनी है।
  _screen: Hypothetical delivery record: 0 → 10 sacks_

**b8 · example · step 2: inputs and the place** — Seller 10 sacks का authorised record भेजता है; application माँगे तो buyer की confirmation जुड़ती है।
  _screen: Seller → authorised proposal: 10 sacks_

**b9 · process_walkthrough · step 3: each step with its cause and its condition** — Validators authorisation और format जाँचते हैं; agreement से entry जुड़ती है। यह delivery की blockchain application है: यानी data सिर्फ cryptocurrency का होना जरूरी नहीं।
  _screen: Check → agreement → accepted 10 | Blockchain ≠ cryptocurrency only_

**b10 · misconception · step 4: what breaks when a step fails** — Viewer का unsupported बदलाव 10 से 20 accepted नहीं होगा। Validation अकेले असल delivery का proof नहीं है; सही reporting चाहिए।
  _screen: 10 → 20 rejected | Valid record ≠ physical proof_

**b11 · process_walkthrough · step 5: the whole run once more at speed** — Proposal, checks, agreement से shared entry बनती है। खुले features पर application बनाने को central owner की permission नहीं चाहिए; कानून और private-network access rules फिर भी लागू हैं।
  _screen: Submit → validate → agree → share | Open development ≠ unrestricted conduct_

**b12 · recap · step 6: recap as a flow** — Delivery record भी checks से जुड़ता है।
  _screen: Goods report → checks → shared record_

### Part c40.q · none
_frame: process: Evaluate three blockchain statements and four combinations. · how: Separate inspection, subject matter and development permission. · when: After the ledger and delivery mechanisms are understood. · conditions: Use the public, permissionless context intended by the question. · atmosphere: UPSC Prelims 2020 statement-combination practice._

**b13 · exam_tip** — 2020 में public permissionless context में 1 और 3 सही; 2 का ‘cryptocurrency only’ गलत। तुम्हारा combination क्या होगा?
  _screen: 1 ✓ 2 ✗ 3 ✓ | Choose the combination_

**b13a · exam_tip** — a गलत है क्योंकि सही statement 3 छूटता है। b भी गलत है क्योंकि गलत 2 जुड़ता है और सही 3 छूटता है।
  _screen: a: missing 3 ✗ | b: includes 2, missing 3 ✗_

**b13b · exam_tip** — c सिर्फ गलत 2 चुनता है। d सही: 1 और 3, यानी shared control और open development।
  _screen: c: 2 only ✗ | d: 1 and 3 only ✓_

### Part c41.2 · process
_frame: process: Trace one hypothetical stock payment under two alternative settlement methods. · how: Authorisation and bank checks precede individual gross or batch net settlement and credit. · when: Individual real-time settlement versus half-hourly batch cycles. · conditions: Valid instructions, system checks and available funds are required. · atmosphere: A shop owner paying a supplier at another bank._

**b14 · example · step 1: the result first (what it makes)** — ₹2,00,000 का stock खरीदने पर क्या होगा? Successful payment में buyer ₹5,00,000 से ₹3,00,000 और supplier ₹1,00,000 से ₹3,00,000 पर आएगा।
  _screen: Hypothetical stock payment ₹2,00,000 | Buyer ₹5,00,000 → ₹3,00,000 | Supplier ₹1,00,000 → ₹3,00,000_

**b15 · example · step 2: inputs and the place** — Buyer bank A से authorise करता है; bank instruction जाँचता है। Supplier bank B में है; इसी दौर में दूसरा customer B से A को ₹50,000 भेजता है।
  _screen: Hypothetical customer instructions: A → B ₹2,00,000 | B → A ₹50,000_

**b16 · process_walkthrough · step 3: each step with its cause and its condition** — पहले तरीके में payments पूरी रकम पर अलग settle होते हैं। दूसरे में batch का bank-level हिसाब ₹2,00,000 − ₹50,000 = ₹1,50,000 है; customers को पूरी रकम मिलती है। पहला RTGS, Real Time Gross Settlement है; दूसरा NEFT, National Electronic Funds Transfer है।
  _screen: Alternative A: separate full-value obligations ₹2,00,000 and ₹50,000 → RTGS | Alternative B: ₹2,00,000 − ₹50,000 = ₹1,50,000 from A to B → NEFT | Customer amounts unchanged_

**b17 · misconception · step 4: what breaks when a step fails** — Checks या funds अधूरे हों, तो settlement अटक सकता है। App notification का मतलब बैंकों का हिसाब पूरा होना नहीं है।
  _screen: Checks + funds required | Notification ≠ interbank settlement_

**b18 · process_walkthrough · step 5: the whole run once more at speed** — Supplier payment में authorisation, checks, bank settlement और beneficiary credit आते हैं; notification अलग पहचानो।
  _screen: Authorise → check → settle → credit | Customer notification is distinct_

**b19 · recap · step 6: recap as a flow** — Gross पूरा अलग; net बाकी रकम है।
  _screen: Gross: full individual obligations | Net: offset bank obligations | No new customer money_

### Part c41.1 · comparison
_frame: process: Compare bank-transfer systems on three independent criteria. · how: Align settlement mode, inward charges and operating availability. · when: Evaluate availability in the question's 2025 setting. · conditions: Inward refers to the recipient; availability is not settlement speed. · atmosphere: A supplier checking receipt of a customer's bank payment._

**b20 · compare · step 1: what is common** — Supplier को दोनों तरीकों से पैसा मिलता है; फर्क कहाँ है? Settlement यानी भुगतान के पीछे बैंकों का आपसी हिसाब पूरा होना।
  _screen: RTGS and NEFT: bank transfers | Compare settlement, inward charges and hours_

**b21 · compare · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — RTGS हर payment real time में अलग settle करता है; NEFT half-hourly batches में। Inward यानी खाते में पैसा आना: दोनों में receiving customer के लिए free है; दोनों 24×7 हैं।
  _screen: RTGS: individual real time | NEFT: half-hourly batches | Both: inward free, 24×7_

**b22 · explain · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — NEFT में batch का इंतजार है; RTGS में नहीं। RBI ने NEFT को 16 December 2019 और RTGS को 14 December 2020 से round-the-clock किया, दोनों 2025 से पहले।
  _screen: NEFT: 16 December 2019 | RTGS: 14 December 2020 → Both precede 2025_

**b23 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — भेजने का charge, पाने का charge नहीं है। 24×7 खुलना भी real-time settlement नहीं है।
  _screen: Sending ≠ receiving | Availability ≠ settlement mode_

**b24 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Settlement अलग; दोनों inward-free और 24×7 हैं।
  _screen: Comparison table: settlement differs | inward receipts free in both | both 24×7_

### Part c41.q · none
_frame: process: Select the correct RTGS–NEFT statement combination. · how: Keep the settlement claim and reject false inward-charge and restricted-hours contrasts. · when: In the supplied 2025 examination context. · conditions: Instantaneous describes the settlement model, not guaranteed zero interface delay. · atmosphere: Prelims statement elimination._

**b25 · exam_tip** — 2025 में I सही; instantaneous real-time model है, zero-delay guarantee नहीं। II का inward charge और III के restricted days गलत हैं।
  _screen: I ✓ II ✗ III ✗_

**b25a · exam_tip** — a सही है: I real-time और batch का फर्क बताता है। b गलत है क्योंकि II पाने वाले पर charge लगाता है।
  _screen: (a) I only ✓ | (b) I and II ✗_

**b25b · exam_tip** — c और d में गलत III है: दोनों 24×7 हैं। d सही I भी छोड़ता है।
  _screen: (c) I and III ✗ | (d) III only ✗_

### Part c42.1 · process
_frame: process: Transfer existing money across UPI-enabled bank accounts using BHIM. · how: Identify the payee, verify the amount, authorise and confirm successful processing. · when: Treat payment as completed after successful status and receipt confirmation. · conditions: Eligible linked accounts, registered-device linkage and PIN authorisation are required. · atmosphere: A kirana purchase where shopper and merchant use different apps._

**b26 · example · step 1: the result first (what it makes)** — Merchant की app अलग हो, तो ₹500 कैसे पहुँचेंगे? Successful खरीद में तुम्हारा balance ₹2,000 से ₹1,500, उसका ₹3,000 से ₹3,500 होगा।
  _screen: Hypothetical grocery payment: ₹500 | Different apps | Balance arrows_

**b27 · example · step 2: inputs and the place** — Merchant का linked account चुनकर नाम और ₹500 जाँचो। Registered device पर UPI PIN से payment authorise करो।
  _screen: Payee + ₹500 check → registered device + UPI PIN_

**b28 · process_walkthrough · step 3: each step with its cause and its condition** — Network और banks processing पूरी करते हैं, तभी balances बदलते हैं। यह BHIM से UPI transfer है; Unified Payments Interface eligible bank accounts जोड़ता है, same app जरूरी नहीं।
  _screen: BHIM → UPI → linked accounts | Existing ₹500 transferred, not created_

**b29 · misconception · step 4: what breaks when a step fails** — Amount भरना काफी नहीं; authorisation या processing अधूरी हो, तो payment completed मत मानो।
  _screen: Amount entered ≠ successful payment_

**b30 · process_walkthrough · step 5: the whole run once more at speed** — खरीद में payee जाँचो, authorise करो, फिर successful status और receipt confirm करो।
  _screen: Verify → authorise → process → confirm_

**b31 · recap · step 6: recap as a flow** — BHIM दरवाजा है, UPI रास्ता; पैसा जुड़े accounts में जाता है।
  _screen: App ≠ network ≠ account_

### Part c42.2 · comparison
_frame: process: Compare authentication evidence in chip-and-PIN and BHIM payments. · how: Distinguish possession and knowledge from transaction screens. · when: During authorisation of the respective purchase. · conditions: Use the ordinary card-plus-PIN and registered-device-plus-UPI-PIN models in the question. · atmosphere: A shopper comparing card and mobile payment._

**b32 · compare · step 1: what is common** — Card लगाना और PIN डालना क्या अलग सबूत हैं? Authentication factors यानी पहचान के अलग तरह के सबूत; card और BHIM दोनों में possession और knowledge हैं।
  _screen: Authentication evidence: possession + knowledge_

**b33 · compare · step 2: criteria one at a time, both sides together** — Card में possession का सबूत card है; BHIM में registered mobile/device linkage। Knowledge दोनों में secret PIN की है।
  _screen: Card + card PIN | Registered device linkage + UPI PIN_

**b34 · analogy · step 3: the reason behind each difference** — Bank को proof चाहिए, clicks नहीं। चाबी और secret code अलग proof हैं; बार-बार चाबी घुमाना नए factors नहीं बनाता।
  _screen: Key possession + code knowledge | Repeated actions ≠ new factors_

**b35 · misconception · step 4: the most confused pair named directly** — Payee, amount और confirmation screens actions हैं, independent factors नहीं। Chip-pin card को four-factor कहना गलत है।
  _screen: Transaction steps ≠ authentication factors | Four-factor premise ✗_

**b36 · recap · step 5: difference table as recap** — Possession अलग; PIN knowledge दोनों में है।
  _screen: Comparison table: card versus device linkage | PIN knowledge shared_

  _screen: 1 ✓ 2 ✗ | Evaluate both halves_

  _screen: (a) 1 only ✓ | (b) 2 only ✗_
